Hard to shut it off.\n\nDoctor: Do you notice that this constant need to keep moving and doing things is worse at certain times of the day, like in the morning or at night, or does it feel about the same all day long?\n\nPatient: It’s… kind of all day, I think. I don’t notice one time being clearly worse. It’s more like once I’m moving, I just keep going—school, home, whatever—and I jump right into the next thing without really shutting off.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any distinct periods of time—maybe a few days or more—where you felt different from your usual self, almost like a shift in your mood, not just being busy or restless?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… I wouldn’t say I’ve noticed a clear shift like that. Like I can’t really shut myself off.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you need less sleep lately, or that you feel rested even if you haven’t slept much?\n\nPatient: Not really. At work it’s gotten bad enough that they put me on a corrective plan, which is… hard to say out loud.\n\nAnd at home, my spouse says the routines—caregiving stuff—are harder because everyone’s kind of managing my energy too. I’m trying to help, but it feels like I’m wearing people down, and maybe damaging trust with them.\n\nDoctor: How often do you feel this constant need to keep moving and doing things—almost every day, or does it come and go more randomly?\n\nPatient: Almost every day, I’d say. It’s not really random. I feel like I’m just… running on a motor most of the time, like I finish one thing and I’m already onto the next. Hard to shut it off.\n\nDoctor: Do you notice that this constant need to keep moving and doing things is worse at certain times of the day, like in the morning or at night, or does it feel about the same all day long?\n\nPatient: It’s… kind of all day, I think. I don’t notice one time being clearly worse. It feels more like I’m just like this most of the time, not a separate few days where I’m suddenly different.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt down, hopeless, or lost interest in things you usually enjoy, even if it was just for a few days?\n\nPatient: Not really… I mean, everyone has off days, but I can’t think of a period where I felt hopeless or stopped caring about things like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any distinct periods of time—maybe four days or more—where you felt unusually high, elated, or irritable, not just being busy or restless?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so.
